### Page 1

From Enterprise AI Adoption to Semicap
Equipment: Sizing the AI Build-out
SEMICON TAIWAN 2026 | MARKET AND INDUSTRY TREND FORUM
Rolf Bulk
Director of Research
RBulk@ETR.ai · +65 6230 3419
September 1, 2026
ABOUT ETR
Enterprise Technology Research (ETR) is an enterprise technology market research firm that delivers
actionable, transparent, and unbiased insights to institutional investors, drawing on proprietary surveys of
a trusted community of nearly 10,000 technology leaders. For more than 15 years, ETR's data has

empowered its clients to make smarter, faster decisions. ETR is a Futurum Group company. Learn more
at etr.ai.
ABOUT THE FUTURUM GROUP
The Futurum Group is a technology research, intelligence, and advisory firm. Futurum Intelligence, the
research arm of The Futurum Group, is led by analysts, researchers, and advisors who help business
leaders worldwide anticipate tectonic shifts in their industries and leverage disruptive innovation. Learn
more at futurumgroup.com.

ENTERPRISE SURVEY DATA AND THE ETR CORE AI MODEL

### Page 2

AI adoption is near-universal, and demand spans multiple vendors.
OUR TAKE ON THE DATA
AI use at enterprises will be ubiquitous before the
end of the decade.
Source: ETR Technology Spending Intentions Survey, July 2026 (N=1,636; 1,024 citing ML/AI); ETR AI Tools Pulse, June to August 2026 (N=100 each).

ML / AI adoption is increasing: pervasion amongst
1,636 enterprises surveyed tripled in four years to
62.6%.
The largest buyers are ahead of the curve: Fortune
100 pervasion is 71%, up 10 points over the year.

No winner takes all dynamic: including pilots,
Claude and Copilot are in use or evaluated by 88%
of enterprises.
Room for higher penetration: Claude is piloted by
35% of enterprises vs. 17% for Copilot, with more of
its base still to convert.

Claude leads on capability: it is well ahead of
peers on accuracy, pace of innovation (4.5) and
reasoning (4.4).
Claude is the preferred tool for 5/6 use cases:

coding, data, and agentic performance, but also
research and writing.
Satisfaction is at a high: Claude reached 4.5 in our
August survey. Peers rank between 3.8 and 4.0.
Cost is increasingly perceived to be an issue:
Claude's perception (change in sentiment) slipped
from 4.0 to 3.7, and it ranks below peers on cost
effectiveness (3.5).

AI leads every sector on intent: a Net Score of 50.5

(adoption plus increased spending) tops all 29
sectors we track.

46%.

Accounts that grew consumption over 50% month-
on-month dropped from 21% in July to 5% in August.

Agentic AI on the rise:68% of enterprises expect a
further shift toward agentic usage over the next
three months.
Growth is driven by more tasks: around 60% name

a higher number of tasks as the largest driver for
growth, with consistent ratings across all tools.
Increasing complexity is a secondary driver: 10%
to 20% cite more tokens per task as the largest
driver of higher consumption.

### Page 6

The ROI on AI is positive for most enterprises, with funding mostly new budget but also reallocations.
OUR TAKE ON THE DATA
Is AI spending tracking in line with expectations, and
where is budget reallocated from?
Source: ETR AI Product Series, July 2026 (N=600); ROI compared with November 2024 (N=600).
REALIZED ROI FROM AI SPENDING (%)
0

November 2024 July 2026
ETR AI Product Series, N=600 each period.

AGREE OR STRONGLY AGREE WITH THE STATEMENT (%)
Net new AI budget 67%
Renew vendor on AI 64%
Reallocate from other tech 61%
Adopt vendor on AI 60%
Improved our edge 57%
Hiring below revenue 50%
Statements posed to IT decision makers on AI budget and vendor choice.
AI returns are improving: positive ROI of 51% in
July, the highest of eight surveys, up from 46% in
late 2024.
Investments are rationalizing: the share of
enterprises not measuring ROI came down from

27% to 20%.
New budget is being allocated:67% of companies
have new AI budget available next year.
AI is prioritized in vendor selection: AI features
drive 64% of renewals and 60% of new deals.
Budget is cut from other tech areas:61% of
enterprises reallocate budget from other areas.

### Page 7

AI budgets are overrunning at close to half of enterprises; consultants and hardware refresh are funding the gap.
OUR TAKE ON THE DATA
How do companies plan to control token over-
consumption?
Source: ETR Macro Views, July 2026 (N=1,636). Over-plan N=767; reallocation N=297.

N=297 who reallocate IT budget to cover AI overruns. Multi-select.
Half the market is over budget:47% run above
plan, 32% in line, and only 6% came in under budget.
Enterprises don't slow down: 48% seek extra
budget and 43% reconcile budgets next cycle. Only
17% pause or scale back AI deployments.

Contractors are cut first: external contractors and
consultants absorb 61% of reallocations.
Hardware refresh is delayed:49% of companies
push out refresh cycles, followed by non-AI
software and headcount.

Base: respondents managing or planning to manage against hard limits (N=37).
Hard limits are still rare: only 17% manage
consumption against hard limits today.
But ambitions are there:37% of enterprises expect

to have hard limits within three months.
Curtailing agentic work is a key cost lever:
restricting agentic workloads and reducing tokens
per task are the joint top methods to control
budgets, at 51% each.

Open-source adoption is one of the key trends to

Pilots (N=47), at comparable performance. Sums to 100%.
Open weights are already in production:47% of
organizations are piloting and 31% run them in
production.
Llama leads, but China is also a major contributor:
Llama tops pilot and production uptake, but

DeepSeek, GLM and Qwen are also frequently
used.
Small discounts can move workloads: at parity in
performance, 57% of pilots would switch for a
discount of 25% or less.
This is even more pronounced for production
workloads: among those already in production,
74% would switch for 25% or less.

Hardware Software Outsourced Cloud
Macro Views, July 2026 (N=1,636). Hardware covers all purchasing categories, AI parts included.
Budget is the main constraint, followed by
memory:41% of buyers name budget as the #1
constraint in deploying more compute; memory is
#2, well ahead of GPUs.

Hardware inflation shows up in enterprise
reporting: hardware pricing up 6.9% half-on-half in
July, from 3.6% in January, while cloud and software
were stable in a 2 to 4% band.
Memory is ahead of other segments:80% of
memory buyers report price increases, and 26% of
those saw 50% or more.

Accelerators CPUs System DRAM System NAND

Total datacenter capex, AI and general purpose. Shares may not sum to 100 because of rounding.
Capex per gigawatt is set to increase: stable at
$35B to $36B in prior years, but set to increase over

10% this year, driven by higher component costs.
Memory repricing: system DRAM goes from 7% of
datacenter capex in 2025 to 20% in 2026, and is
unlikely to come down materially.
CPUs are a smaller moving part: set to increase
from $64B this year to over $250B in 2030, driven
primarily by standalone deployments.
Total capex can exceed $4T in 2030:2.8x 2026
spending, with close to $1.3T of it on accelerators.
SEMICON TAIWAN 2026 | MARKET AND INDUSTRY TREND FORUM

A gigawatt needs 745,000 wafers of leading-
edge silicon on average: across the GPU, server
rack, and related networking.
Memory dominates the build-up: HBM, DRAM and
NAND take over 75% of the wafers per GW.
$6B of WFE required per GW: of which DRAM and
HBM $3.5B, logic $1.3B, with NAND and CoWoS the

remainder.

Capacity requirement: 75 GW requires over 550k
WSPM of advanced logic, over 2m of DRAM and

backside power and new materials in logic, more
EUV layers in DRAM, and higher layer counts in
NAND.
Cumulative spending required:$573B of WFE for
AI servers, excluding standalone CPU deployments
and related storage.

GPU and ASIC vendors lock in customer

commitments years ahead: Nvidia, Broadcom and
AMD all focus on multi-year volume agreements to
anchor their roadmap.
Memory vendors follow suit: the leading memory
vendors now have 50% to 70% of capacity locked
in under 3 to 5 year agreements with floor pricing.
Equipment sector remains largely capacity driven:
service revenue is 23% of total sector revenue in
2026, down from 27% in 2019.

聯發科 (2454 TT)  Buy

輝達認購聯發科 ECB，將帶動聯發科短期評價，投資建議
給 Buy。  
投資建議 ：輝達 (NVDA US) 認購聯發科 ECB ，有助於短期評價
與長期營運，投資建議給 Buy 。  
輝達認購聯發科 ECB：輝達宣布斥資 35 億美元，認購聯發科的
ECB ，其餘 ECB 認購公司包含 Alphabet(GOOG US) 。  
聯發科將資金用於後續 AI 商機：聯發科此次募資資金將用於多
項用途，除了強化全球供應鏈外，也將持續加碼晶片設計、
ASIC 、互連 IP 以及先進封裝能力，藉此支援後續更大型的 AI
專案。  
雙方將有更多合作： 未來加強合作領域包含以下三點。一、 AI
基礎建設，聯發科將會基於 NVLink Fusion 生態系，協助客戶
開發基礎建設，並整合進 NVIDIA 的機櫃及 AI 工廠。二、邊緣
AI 運算，雙方會開發更多 RTX Spark 與 DGX Spark 的電腦晶
片。三、車用，雙方將持續開發具備 AI 功能的軟體定義汽車平
台。  
輝達投資聯發科，有助於聯發科 XPU 商機： 聯發科已與部分潛
在客戶展開接洽， NVIDIA 直接投資聯發科，可讓客戶更願意採
用聯發科 XPU 搭配 NVLink Fusion 的整體方案，也可讓雙方擴
大供應鏈與產品版圖。

### Page 2

個股報告  
                                        2026 年 9 月 1 日

2 群益投顧 
  ESG ：  
環境保護：聯發科技做為全球半導體產業技術領導者，在整體價值鏈中位於
最前端的晶片設計，後端的原物料採購、代工製造、封裝與測試等環節皆交
由供應商執行，形成專業分工與互相合作的夥伴關係。根據風險調查與碳足
跡盤查發現，主要對社會與環境產生衝擊影響的關鍵在於後端製造作業，故
如何與供應商共同合作，以降低社會與環境相關的潛在風險，為聯發科技發
展綠色營運的首要任務。  
社會責任：運用數位科技解決社會問題不是企業的專利，聯發科技相信每個
人都有潛力利用數位科技創造無限可能，聯發科發起數位社會創新競賽 「智
在家鄉」，鼓勵社會大眾為家鄉問題提供解方。  
公司治理：聯發科技董事會下設有審計委員會，強化公司治理獨立性，並設
立薪資報酬委員會，以健全公司薪酬制度為目標，持續強化企業永續發展。
除前述法定功能性委員會外，本公司亦設立法定以外之併購策略委員會，視
實際需求針對集團具體併購案件及整體併購策略評估分析。  
產品組合：  
2Q26 產品組合：手機晶片業務 41% 、智慧裝置平台業務 53% 、 PMIC 6% 。  
手機業務全年展望保守：  
2Q26 手機晶片業務營收 QoQ-14%\YoY-20% ，營收占比 41% ，衰退原因是
手機的 BOM 表的成本上漲，聯發科預計 2026 年全球手機出貨量 YoY 約 -
15% 。  
客戶重視旗艦級產品與價格敏感的入門級產品 ， 聯發科 3Q26 將推出 2nm 製
程的 SoC ，為下一代 AI 模型提供動力，聯發科與全球廠商合作，持續擴大
2027 年旗艦級手機的市占率。  
供應鏈成本上漲已經是產業現象，聯發科將對價格進行調整，以確保成本上
漲可以反映在產品定價中。 3Q26 手機業務展望季持平或季減中個位數百分
比，旗艦級 SoC 的量產可以抵消大部分其他手機業務的衰退。  
客戶重視旗艦級產品與價格敏感的入門級產品 ， 聯發科 3Q26 將推出 2nm 製
程的 SoC ，為下一代 AI 模型提供動力，聯發科與全球廠商合作，持續擴大
2027 年旗艦級手機的市占率。  
智慧邊緣平台業務展望正向：  
2Q26 智慧邊緣平台業務營收 QoQ+19\YoY+26% ，營收占比 53%。成長動能
包含在運算、車用的市佔率的提升，以及 TV SoC 的成長。多項連接、車用
項目走向量產，聯發科預計此業務將季增中高個位數營收。  
2Q26 PMIC 營收占比約 6%：  
2Q26 PMIC 業務營收 QoQ+11%\YoY+6% ，營收占比 6%。成長動能包含資
料中心的市占率提升，聯發科預計此業務將季持平。

### Page 3

個股報告  
                                        2026 年 9 月 1 日

3 群益投顧 
AI ASIC 業務展望更樂觀：  
聯發科第一個 AI 加速器 ASIC 將於 4Q26 開始量產，預計 2026 全年資料中
心將貢獻超過 20 億美元，並且在 2027 年倍數成長，聯發科預計 AI ASIC 在
2027 年的市場規模將達到 800 億美元，聯發科目標市佔率從 10~15% ，上修
至 15~20% 。第二個 AI 加速器 ASIC 將於 2028 年初量產，聯發科有信心在
2028 年的市占率將提升。  
聯發科認為每代的 ASIC 的毛利率相當，並不會更高， ASIC 的毛利率低於
目前公司平均，但新世代的 ASIC 對營收的貢獻貴更大。  
其他補充：  
聯發科 448G SerDes 的研發順利，採用 CPO 的解決方案。 448G SerDes 之
後，聯發科會在台積電 (2330 TT) 的 COUPE 平台上，繼續發展 CPO 的解決
方案。聯發科還有提供 3.5D 的平台，該平台提供 IP、封裝與設計流程，適
用於大型資料中心的設計。  
供應鏈成本上漲已經是產業現象，聯發科將對價格進行調整，以確保成本上
漲可以反映在產品定價中。聯發科目前還在投入新技術，絕對費用金額還會
再增加，但是由於營收規模上升，費用率會下降。

7 群益投顧 
  【投資評等說明】  
  評等  定義  
  強力買進 (Strong Buy)  首次評等潛在上漲空間 ≥ 35%  
  買進 (Buy)  15% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <35%  
  區間操作 (Trading Buy) 5% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <15%

中立 (Neutral)  
無法由基本面給予投資評等  
預期近期股價將處於盤整  
建議降低持股

【投資評等調整機制】  
  投資評等係「首次」給予特定個股投資評等時，其前一日收盤價相較 3 個月
目標價之潛在上漲空間計算而得。個股投資評等分為四個等級，定義如上。
爾後的投資評等係依循「首次評等」，直到停止推薦。   
「強力買進」、「買進」及「區間操作」均有 upside 目標價。差別僅在於，
首次評等時潛在報酬率不同。「中立」則無目標價。  
一旦我們給予特定個股「強力買進」 、 「買進」或「區間操作」之投資評等，
就是責任的開始，爾後將透過各式研究報告作定期性、持續性追蹤基本面及
股價變化，直到停止推薦。  
停止推薦情境：  
1.  達目標價。  
2.  雖未達原訂目標價，但檢視基本面、訊息面、籌碼面等多方訊息，研
判股價上漲空間已然有限，將適時出具降評報告。  
3.  推薦後股價不漲反跌。

【免責聲明】  
  本研究報告僅提供予特定人之客戶作為參考資料「非經同意不得轉載」 。我
們並不確保此資訊的完整性與正確性，投資人應了解，報告中有關未來預測
之陳述可能不會實現，因而不應被依賴。而且此報告並非根據特定投資目的
或依預定對象之財務狀況所撰寫出來的，因此，此研究報告的目的，既非對
投資人於買賣證券、選擇權、期貨或其他證券相關之衍生性商品提供詢價服
務，亦非作為進行交易的要約。投資人應注意到相關證券之價值及收益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

ab 31 August 2026
Global Research
First Read
Key Call: MediaTek Inc.
Expanding partnership with Nvidia and Google
for AI computing platforms
What happened?
MediaTek announced the successful pricing of a US$3.9 billion overseas convertible
bond (CB) offering, with NVIDIA investing US$3.5 billion and the remainder subscribed
by Alphabet (Google) and leading global institutional investors, underscoring strong
confidence in the company’s AI strategy and expansion from custom AI silicon into

broader AI systems and data center platforms. Alongside the financing, MediaTek and
NVIDIA unveiled an expanded strategic partnership spanning AI infrastructure, local AI
computing, and automotive applications. A key highlight is MediaTek’s adoption of
NVIDIA’s NVLink Fusion platform, enabling customers to develop custom AI accelerators
that integrate with NVIDIA’s rack-scale AI factories. The collaboration will also extend
across multiple generations of AI PC chips, including RTX Spark and DGX Spark

platforms, as well as AI-powered software-defined vehicle solutions, further
strengthening MediaTek’s position across the AI value chain from edge to cloud.
Our view
We are optimistic about MediaTek's competitive positioning and opportunity in Cloud
and Edge AI. The partnership showcases Nvidia’s ongoing push to sell its NVLINK fusion
(including scale up photonics or electrical chiplet, C2C interconnect, and custom
NVHBM base die) into Cloud ASICs. Meanwhile, with compelling technology portfolio

of power efficient SoC, connectivity and advanced packaging, MediaTek could act as a
powerful partner of Nvidia to scale the AI solutions to the edge. Edge AI is only at the
beginning of development but could generate substantial upside in the next 5-10 years
across PC, automotive, robotics and others, which MediaTek and Nvidia could develop
industry leading solutions together. The expanded partnership with Nvidia could also
elevate MediaTek's technology capabilities for AI solutions. Separately for Google's TPU,

our view is the ASIC TAM is growing quickly in the next 2-3 years, and MediaTek should
expand its share from high 20% in 2027 to 40-45% with upside in 2028, based on TPU
die units. Google's effort in accelerating the new LLM development should support
robust demand for TPU v8t (training) designed by MediaTek in 2027. New generation
TPU like v10 coming in 2029-30 with chiplet structure may open up opportunity for
other ASIC vendors, but MediaTek should remain the main compute ASIC supplier given

the increasing integration complexity and recognize major portion of ASIC service
revenue, in our view.
Valuation: reiterate Key Call Buy and target of NT$6,500
Our target price is based on 27x 2027-28E PE. Maintain Key Call Buy rating.

Company Description
MediaTek is a leading integrated circuit (IC) design company in Taiwan, focusing on solutions
from the edge to the cloud, applications for its product offerings range from smartphones,
smart homes and AI PCs to high-performance computing, automotive, and AI data centers.

Valuation Method and Risk Statement
Our price target for MediaTek is based on a target PE methodology.
Fabless integrated circuit (IC) design houses face a variety of uncertainties, including rapidly
changing technology, end-market demand, competition, employee turnover and foundry
wafer costs. They are also vulnerable to legal disputes over intellectual property rights. In
addition, MediaTek is exposed to potential price competition in handset SoC, TV SoC, etc.

Downside risks include slower-than-expected market share gains and a weaker-than-
expected demand recovery.

Investing in structured investments involves significant risks. For a detailed discussion of the risks involved in investing in any particular structured investment, you must read

the relevant offering materials for that investment. Structured investments are unsecured obligations of a particular issuer with returns linked to the performance of an
underlying asset. Depending on the terms of the investment, investors could lose all or a substantial portion of their investment based on the performance of the underlying
asset. Investors could also lose their entire investment if the issuer becomes insolvent. UBS does not guarantee in any way the obligations or the financial condition of any

issuer or the accuracy of any financial information provided by any issuer. Structured investments are not traditional investments and investing in a structured investment is
not equivalent to investing directly in the underlying asset. Structured investments may have limited or no liquidity, and investors should be prepared to hold their investment
to maturity. The return of structured investments may be limited by a maximum gain, participation rate or other feature. Structured investments may include call features and,

if a structured investment is called early, investors would not earn any further return and may not be able to reinvest in similar investments with similar terms. Structured
investments include costs and fees which are generally embedded in the price of the investment. The tax treatment of a structured investment may be complex and may differ
from a direct investment in the underlying asset. UBS and its employees do not provide tax advice. Investors should consult their own tax advisor about their own tax situation

before investing in any securities.

Airtac
Valuation
We derive our target price of NT$1,750 for Airtac by using a P/E methodology, as we believe the margin and earnings growth
outlooks will be the key drivers of the stock. We apply a P/E of 27.5x for 2H26-1H27E, which we derive from the mid range of
prior upcycle valuation 25-35x during 2021 in view of a strong upcycle with solid FA demand and ongoing share gains.
Risks
Key downside risks that could impede the Airtac stock from reaching our target price include: 1) a softer-than-expected China

macro economy, resulting in downside to our sales forecasts; 2) intensifying competition leading to gross margin pressures;
and 3) slower-than-expected progress of its linear guide business, causing low utilization of capacity.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds

our ratings distribution table for our Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside rating system. However, we reiterate that we do
not consider No View to be a recommendation. For all Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside calls, risk exists that the
catalyst(s) and associated share-price movement will not materialize as expected.

### Page 3

Hon Hai (2317.TW; Buy, on CL): Jul rev in line at +54% YoY; AI server and smartphones to support 3Q 
growth

Hon Hai’s Jul revenues were +54% YoY/ +15% MoM to NT$947m, or 2% higher than our 
estimates, overall in line. From a MoM perspective, Consumer Electronics & 
Components delivered significant growth MoM, benefitting from the pull-in 
momentum, followed by Cloud and Networking & Computing which achieved strong 
growth MoM. From a YoY perspective, all segments across Consumer Electronics, 
Components, Cloud and Networking & Computing delivered strong growth YoY, and 
management attributed this to the strong pull-in momentum and higher selling prices of 
Consumer Electronics and Computing products. We expect 3Q26E revenues to be +48% 
YoY / +22% QoQ to NT$3.1 trillion. June/ July delivered strong YoY (+52%/ 54% YoY), 
and we expect Aug revenues to remain strong at 73% YoY / 11% MoM to NT$1,051bn, 
supported by AI rack server ramp-up, and strong seasonality of the consumer 
electronics business. Maintain Buy (on CL).

Price Target Risks and Methodology – Hon Hai 
Valuation methodology: We are Buy rated on Hon Hai. Our 12-month target price of 
NT$400 is based on a 18.9x 2027E P/E multiple, which is set in line with peers’ PEG&M 
ratio (i.e., P/E vs. forward-year earnings growth and OPM). 
Key downside risks: (1) slower-than-expected ramp-up of the AI server business; (2) 
weaker-than-expected EV total solution performance across EV assembly, design, 
software, and semis; (3) slower-than-expected ramp-up of capacity globally; and (4) 
fiercer-than-expected competition in the consumer electronics EMS business.

Quanta (2382.TW, Neutral): AI server in strong ramp up; PC to decline in 2H26 on high memory cost

Quanta’s Jul revenue was down 5% QoQ / up 131% YoY, or 21% higher than our 
estimate, which we attribute to the strong rack-level AI server shipments, along with the 
stronger than expected PC shipments at 4.5m units. Looking ahead, we expect 
sequential revenue decline in Aug-Sep, mainly on the memory price hike weighing on PC 
end demand. We model sequential revenue increases in Oct-Dec, supported by the next 
generation rack-level AI server shipments ramp up, which carries a higher ASP . Overall, 
we model Quanta’s 3Q / 4Q26E revenues to grow at 85% / 47% YoY, riding on the 
growing AI infrastructure trend. Maintain Neutral.

11x 2027E P/E, which references PC/server peers’ EPS growth vs. P/E multiple 
correlation. We see EPS growth as a major factor for the stock’s performance.  
Key upside/downside risks include: 1) stronger-/weaker-than-expected PC market 
recovery; 2) faster-/slower-than-expected AI server ramp-up; and 3) 
stronger-/weaker-than-expected demand for general servers.

AVC (3017.TW, Buy): Liquid cooling component ASP and GM upside on the rising complexity of server 
racks; July in line

AVC’s Jul revenue was up 57% YoY / 6% QoQ to NT$19bn, largely in line with our 
estimate. We attribute the strong YoY growth to the strong shipments of AI server liquid 
cooling components, riding on the increasing AI server end demand and a growing liquid 
cooling penetration rate. We model sequential revenue growth in Aug-Dec, considering 
(1) rising shipments of next-generation rack-level AI servers, (2) an increase in liquid 
cooling dollar content in next-generation AI server racks given their growing complexity, 
and (3) a rising liquid cooling penetration rate in both ASIC and GPU AI servers. Maintain 
Buy.

Price Target Risks and Methodology - AVC 
We are Buy-rated on AVC. Our 12-month target price for AVC is based on a near-term 
P/E, consistent with our Taiwan Technology coverage. We base our target price of 
NT$3,986 on a target P/E multiple of 28.2x applied to our forward-year EPS (2027E). 
Our target P/E is derived from the correlation between P/E and EPS growth of its peers 
in technology hardware.  
Key downside risks: (1) slower-than-expected adoption of liquid cooling; (2) 
lower-than-expected demand for AI servers; (3) fiercer-than-expected competition; 
and (4) a slower-than-expected recovery in general servers.

Wistron (3231.TW, Buy): July and 2Q26 beat; AI server rack in a strong ramp-up with customer 
expansion and specification upgrades

Wistron’s July revenue was up 61% YoY to NT$308bn, or 30% higher than our estimate, 
reflecting strong AI server rack demand from neo-cloud and enterprises, offsetting the 
impact of the model transition. We expect sequential revenue growth ahead through 
Dec 2026, driven by new GPU AI server racks entering mass production in 4Q26, which 
will drive up the ASP , along with strong AI spending and the expansion of company’s 
customer base. The new GPU AI server racks have various racks across GPU, CPU, and 
LPU, supporting ODM growth in coming quarters. On PC, the company’s July shipments 
were at 1.6m units (-20% MoM / YoY), reflecting modest demand under high memory 
costs. Despite PC shipments likely being negative HoH considering the strong pull-in 
since late 2025, we expect the strong AI server rack ramp-up to offset the impact, 
driving 2H26 revenue up +28% HoH. Maintain Buy.

Price Target Risks and Methodology - Wistron 
Our 12-month target price of NT$281 is based on a 13.1x 2027E P/E, which is derived 
from PC/server peer EPS growth vs. P/E multiple correlation. We see EPS growth as a 
major factor for the stock’s share price performance.   
Key downside risks: 1) weaker-than-expected PC market recovery, 2) 
slower-than-expected AI server demand ramp-up, and 3) weaker-than-expected 
demand on general servers.

than our estimate, reflecting AI servers shipment ramp up. Jul PC shipments was down

the company’s rising AI server shipments, AI server capacity expansion, and PC product 
mix upgrade towards high end model (e.g. AI PC). Overall, we expect Compal’s 3Q /

supported by global capacity expansion in the US, Taiwan, and Vietnam, while consumer 
electronics still accounts for the majority of the company’s total revenue, which demand 
could be capped by rising raw material costs (e.g., memory, CPU). Maintain Neutral on 
fair valuation.

vs. P/E multiple correlation. We see EPS growth and market re-rating for ODM

Pegatron (4938.TW, Sell): AI server driving 2H26 growth; PC growth capped by rising memory cost; Sell

result and raise our 2026-28E net incomes by 10% / 8% / 7%, mainly on higher revenues 
and lower opex ratios. We raise our 2026-28E revenues by 5% / 5% / 4%, mainly on 
higher contribution from AI server business. Our 2026-28E opex ratios are slightly 
revised up, mainly on higher R&D expense to support the AI server specification

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions
Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not Covered), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.
Coverage Universe: A list of all stocks in each coverage universe is available by primary analyst, stock and coverage universe at

https://www.gs.com/research/hedge.html.

Goldman Sachs is acting in an advisory capacity in a merger or in a strategic transaction involving this company, when there are legal, regulatory or
policy constraints due to Goldman Sachs’ involvement in a transaction, and in certain other circumstances. Early-Stage Biotech (ES). An investment

rating and a target price are not assigned pursuant to Goldman Sachs policy when this company has neither a drug, treatment or medical device that

### Page 1

•Broad-base 2Q, cost-capped margin. Mgmt. reiterated that 2Q26 growth 
was broad-based across every product line, end market and region, not a 
single AI pocket. GPM was slightly below Street, as raw material cost 
inflation offset part of the pricing and mix improvement. Internal 
manufacturing efficiency projects are underway to absorb it in the future.
•Jul price hike hits spot and direct. The Jul price adjustment covers spot 
prices and direct customers, vs. earlier rounds limited to contract 
customers, and spans c.50% of revenue including MLCC, tantalum and 
other capacitors. Spot pricing flows through immediately rather than at 
renewal, so mgmt. expects ASP and revenue effects shortly, with margin 
impact far more visible in 2H. Mgmt. also sees no sign of demand slowing 
down in the near-term.
•Tantalum now the largest line. AI is not an MLCC-only story for passive 
components. Tantalum grew fastest of any line in 2Q, suits AI power 
management, and has over taken magnetics to become Yageo's largest 
product line. MLCC also grew well and should keep growing in coming 
quarters. AI revenue is now 16% of total revenue, spread across tantalums, 
MLCCs, resistors and inductors rather than single product.
•65 sites, dual-region by product. Every product is manufactured in at least 
two regions, giving customers flexibility against tariff and geopolitical risks. 
Mgmt. believe the existing 65 facilities are flexible in adding new lines at a 
fast pace whenever shortages appear, saving customers 1-2 years vs. 
building from greenfield. The company also planned the capacity expansion 
ahead of demand.

### Page 2

Yageo (2327.TW)
04 September 2026 Citi Research
2
•YFIII and overseas MLCC bases. YFIII in Kaohsiung, commissioned in 2024, is Yageo's largest MLCC fab and still has
space for additional MLCC lines, with AI-specific equipment arriving monthly since last year. Outside Taiwan, Yageo
has been opening new lines within existing facilities in Mexico and China, leaving it able to absorb incremental AI
orders without a new building cycle.
•Lead times extend, LTA interest rises. Lead time of most of the products has lengthened vs. 1H26. Tantalum is the

tightest, followed by MLCC and resistors. Mgmt. indicated all AI-related lines are extremely tight, and more
customers are now approaching Yageo to sign LTAs.
•NP0 leadership on the power side. Yageo estimates a meaningful share (at least 30-40%) of NP0 high-voltage
MLCC, based on the internal assessment. Built on EV and industrial experience, NP0 fits AI's high-voltage, high-
temp duty and sits on the DC power side, while high-cap MLCC serves the near-chip stage. Yageo ships direct to

leading power supply players and is invited as first batch of design partners.
•Low-to-high conversion only partial. Converting low-end MLCC capacity to high-end is only partially possible.
High-spec MLCC stores more capacitance through higher layer counts, requiring advanced stackers rather than
conventional equipment. Yageo can optimize and redeploy some existing tooling, but not fully substitute, which is
why it is committing capex to new AI production line instead of relying on conversion.

Yageo
Valuation
We set our target price at NT$1,450, based on 35x average 2027E-28E earnings. We value Yageo based on PE as we believe the
company’s earnings performance and industry dynamics would be the key focuses of the market. Our target multiple of 35x is
at the higher end of the trading range since 2022. During this period the company has gone through the cycles, successfully
integrated the acquired business to enhance its product offerings, and started to deliver stable margins and earnings.

Risks
Key downside risks to our target price and rating include: 1) slower-than-expected demand recovery; 2) intensified peer and
pricing competition; 3) execution risks associated with M&A integration and synergies; and 4) FX and interest rate fluctuations.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be

our ratings distribution table for our Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside rating system. However, we reiterate that we do
not consider No View to be a recommendation. For all Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside calls, risk exists that the
catalyst(s) and associated share-price movement will not materialize as expected.

EV business in August 2026: Hon Hai’s EV subsidiary Foxtron (2258.TW, not covered) 
reported Aug revenues of NT$914mn (vs. Jul-26 / Aug-25 revenues at NT$598mn / 
NT$111mn). Post the announcement of its proposal to acquire Luxgen in December 
(report), Foxtron has announced its first self-branded vehicle “Foxtron Bria”. Also, the 
partnership with Mitsubishi Motors (7211.T, covered by Kota Yuzawa) on passenger cars 
(read more in our report) and zero emission buses (read more on the company’s 
announcement) suggests scope for incremental growth opportunities for Foxtron which 
could be positive for Hon Hai.

Earnings revision: We factor in Hon Hai Aug revenues and revise down our 2026E 
earnings by 1%, mainly on lower revenues; meanwhile we remain positive on gradual AI 
server ramp up and smartphones spec upgrade to drive growth ahead. We keep 2027E/ 
2028E estimates largely unchanged.

historical forward P/E since 2010, reflecting our positive view on Hon Hai’s business

Price Target Risks and Methodology – Hon Hai 
Valuation methodology: We are Buy rated on Hon Hai. Our 12-month target price of 
NT$400 is based on an 18.9x 2027E P/E multiple, which is set in line with peers’ PEG&M 
ratio (i.e., P/E vs. forward-year earnings growth and OPM). 
Key downside risks: (1) slower-than-expected ramp-up of the AI server business; (2) 
weaker-than-expected EV total solution performance across EV assembly, design, 
software, and semis; (3) slower-than-expected ramp-up of capacity globally; and (4) 
fiercer-than-expected competition in the consumer electronics EMS business.

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions
Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not Covered), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.
Coverage Universe: A list of all stocks in each coverage universe is available by primary analyst, stock and coverage universe at

https://www.gs.com/research/hedge.html.

Chroma Ate Inc.  | Asia Pacific
August  Sales  +4% MoM/+156%
YoY
In this report, we focus on Chroma's monthly sales, which we
believe could be a catalyst for share price movement.
Details:
• August revenue was NT$5,632mn (+4% MoM/+156% YoY).
• QTD revenue reached 75% of MSe, at NT$14,772mn (+9% QoQ/+130% YoY),
and 79% of consensus, at NT$13,992mn (+3% QoQ/+118% YoY).
Our view:
• We see 3Q26 tracking ahead of market expectations and likely to deliver

over 20% QoQ.
• Looking ahead, we expect its power testing business to stay at an elevated
level, and the semiconductor & photonics business to continue growing HoH
into 2H26, driven by SLT, metrology, CPO and potential shipment of FT
handlers/burn-in ovens.
• Stay OW.

Morgan Stanley Research 3
Valuation Methodology and Risks
Chroma Ate Inc. (2360.TW)
Base case, 45x 2027e P/E. We use P/E as our valuation methodology because we think it better
captures Chroma's earnings growth momentum. The stock traded at an average valuation of
~19x P/E during 2014-25 with an operating profit CAGR of 20%, implying a PEG of 1.0. We think
our target multiple of 45x is justified by the 61% operating profit CAGR we project for

2025-28.
Risks to Upside
n Better-than-expected macro conditions
n Stronger semi and photonics and power testing demand
n Margin improvement via better product mix
n Better opex control
Risks to Downside
n Macro slowdown
n Softer-than-expected AI capex
n Slower progress in the advanced packaging capacity expansion
n Weaker consumer electronics and automobile related demand
n Margin contraction arising from lack of revenue scale and increasing opex

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

Yageo (2327.TW)
08 September 2026 Citi Research
3
Yageo
Valuation
We set our target price at NT$1,450, based on 35x average 2027E-28E earnings. We value Yageo based on PE as we believe the
company’s earnings performance and industry dynamics would be the key focuses of the market. Our target multiple of 35x is
at the higher end of the trading range since 2022. During this period the company has gone through the cycles, successfully
integrated the acquired business to enhance its product offerings, and started to deliver stable margins and earnings.

Risks
Key downside risks to our target price and rating include: 1) slower-than-expected demand recovery; 2) intensified peer and
pricing competition; 3) execution risks associated with M&A integration and synergies; and 4) FX and interest rate fluctuations.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be

our ratings distribution table for our Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside rating system. However, we reiterate that we do
not consider No View to be a recommendation. For all Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside calls, risk exists that the
catalyst(s) and associated share-price movement will not materialize as expected.

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions
Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not Covered), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions
Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not Covered), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.
Coverage Universe: A list of all stocks in each coverage universe is available by primary analyst, stock and coverage universe at

https://www.gs.com/research/hedge.html.
Not Rated (NR). The investment rating, target price and earnings estimates (where relevant) are removed pursuant to Goldman Sachs policy when
Goldman Sachs is acting in an advisory capacity in a merger or in a strategic transaction involving this company, when there are legal, regulatory or
policy constraints due to Goldman Sachs’ involvement in a transaction, and in certain other circumstances. Early-Stage Biotech (ES). An investment

rating and a target price are not assigned pursuant to Goldman Sachs policy when this company has neither a drug, treatment or medical device that
has passed a Phase II clinical trial nor a license to distribute a post-Phase II drug, treatment or medical device.  Rating Suspended (RS). Goldman Sachs
Research has suspended the investment rating and price target for this stock, because there is not a sufficient fundamental basis for determining an
investment rating or target price. The previous investment rating and target price, if any, are no longer in effect for this stock and should not be relied

upon.  Coverage Suspended (CS). Goldman Sachs has suspended coverage of this company.  Not Covered (NC). Goldman Sachs does not cover this
company.

Document 098: 260908_gs_semicon-TW

Report source: line報告備份

### Page 1

1. Photonics IC scaling up: AI drives data transmission speed and bandwidth 
requirement, leading to strong demand on Photonics IC, requesting it to catch up 
Electrical IC automated production and testing ecosystem in 1 to 2 years, while EIC / 
semiconductors has been developed for decades. The strong demand brings 
opportunities to tool suppliers, across coupling and testing, which are necessary 
tools to accelerate PIC production. Robotechnik / FiconTEC describes the numbers of 
tools they target to ship in coming 1 year would be over 50% of what they’ve shipped 
in past 25 years, reflecting the strong demand and orders visibility.  
2. Testing - CPO earliest beneficiary: As a new technology, CPO naturally takes time 
and faces challenges in the path of commercialization; however, we identify testing 
equipment suppliers as the earliest beneficiaries, considering the expensive final 
module means multiple testing during the production process is necessary, given the 
earlier a failure is found, the lower cost a failure is. As a result, companies we visited 
continue to see Insertion 2 (double sided wafer-level testing) as necessary, and 
Robotechnik / FiconTEC launched the new generation in 3Q26, which fully complied 
with the global leading foundry’s communication infrastructure. 
3. Testing - shortening time with KGD (know good die) at early stage: To facilitate 
photonics IC scaling up, not only are more tools needed but better tools, requiring 
suppliers’ strong R&D and execution, delivering continuous specification upgrade. 
Robotechnik / FiconTEC, as global leaders in PIC testing, aim to shorten the 
wafer-level testing time by 50-60% next year, and target 4x faster die-level testing 
next year. Enlitech (7728.TWO, Not Covered) offers NightJar, a hyperspectral image 
system, revealing AWG (arrayed waveguide grating) optical loss pathways with 
absolute optical power mapping, allowing customers to KGD at Insertion 1 (PIC wafer

### Page 2

testing) or Insertion 0 (PIC wafer early defect screening), saving failure costs. 
4. Coupling - automated production becoming necessary in high-speed connection: 
AI drives optical module towards high speed and high density / bandwidth, driving 
demand on automated production. GMT (4573.TWO, Not Covered) showcased dual FA 
active alignment equipment for 1.6T optical module, which could do the coupling for 
both Tx and Rx simultaneously, saving 50% of coupling time compared to traditional 
equipment. FitTech (6706.TW, Not Covered) showcased optical engine assembly system, 
featuring with passive / active alignment and customized development of coupling 
algorithms, which could be used in multiple products: high-speed optical modules, ELS, 
OCS, NPO, CPO, SiPh and optical chiplets. ASMPT showcased MEGA multi-chip bonding 
solution for complex multi-chip and photonics packaging applications, combining 
high-precision placement with adhesive dispensing, UV fixation and 3D inspection in one 
platform. 
5. FAU & Laser specification upgrade: With optical networking expanding from scale 
out to scale up, FAU and laser specification is also migrating. We estimate FOCI‘s FAU in 
2026E to be mainly 40 channels (starting in 3Q26) for 3.2T, followed by 80 channels 
(within 2026E) for 6.4T, and 100 channels under R&D. The company’s FAU enjoys several 
customers across fabless, foundry, laser suppliers, etc. FOCI Aug revenues accelerated to 
+20% MoM (vs. +9% MoM in Jul), reflecting the ramp up of high-end FAU. VPEC‘s 
optoelectronics revenues enjoy strong growth on the back of strong CW lasers demand 
and InP substrate supply getting better. Its InP epiwafer for CW lasers is also migrating 
from 70mW / 100mW (scale out) to above 100mW (scale up, NPO) and 300mW / 
400mW (scale up, CPO), along with expansion from CW lasers to 100G / 200G EML, 
supporting its growth ahead. 
6. AI drives CMP slurry: Anji Micro owns a comprehensive customer base, allowing it to 
ride on growing AI spending across China and overseas. The global-tier AI infrastructure 
continues to migrate, driving the CMP slurry consumption per system, along with AI 
server racks ramp up, enlarging the total addressable market. As the CMP slurry local 
leader, we are positive on Anji’s capability to offer qualified and reliable semi material 
products to penetrate global-tier clients.   
Company highlights

Robotechnik (300757.SZ, Buy, 12m TP at Rmb796) Chairman & Head of FiconTest 
visit: We hosted Robotechnik Chairman and Head of FiconTest and visited the booth in 
Semicon Taiwan on Sep 3. Management of Robotechnik highlights the strong demand on 
Photonics IC automated production and testing, driving their tools demand. The 
company’s strong beat in 2Q26 results (report link) reaffirms our positive view on 
Robotechnik, riding on the growing trend across CPO, NPO, OCS, and optical modules. 
We identify Robotechnik to be the major beneficiary in the photonics IC scaling trend, 
considering its comprehensive tools coverage and strong automation capability across 
software and hardware. Read more: Robotechnik / FiconTEC Semicon Taiwan 
takeaways.

Taiwan takeaways.
Price target methodology and risks - ASMPT
Valuation methodology: We are Neutral-rated on ASMPT with a 12-month target price
of HK$177, which is based on 24.9x 2030E discounted P/E (discounted back to 2027E).
Our target P/E is derived from correlation between peers P/E to the sum of NI YoY and
OPM.
Key upside/downside risks: 1) customers’ faster-/slower-than-expected adoption of
Advanced Packaging tools; 2) stronger-/weaker-than-expected demand from

automotive customers; and 3) stronger-/weaker-than-expected demand for traditional
IC packaging and SMT equipment.
Price Target Risks and Methodology - Anji Micro
Valuation: We are Buy-rated on Anji Micro with a 12-month target price of Rmb509,
which is based on 46x 2030E discounted P/E (discounted back to 2027E). Our target P/E
is derived from correlation between peers P/E to the sum of NI YoY and OPM.
Key downside risks: 1) supply chain risks; 2) weaker semi client demand; and 3)

weaker-than-expected product expansion.
Valuation: Our 12-month target price of NT$833 is based on a 15.4x 2030E P/E,
discounted back to 2027E at a COE of 12.6%. Our target multiple is derived from peers’
correlation between forward-year trading P/E and earnings growth.
Key downside risks: Fiercer-than-expected market competition, technology migration
trends in optical networking, and slower-than-expected capacity ramp-up.
Price Target Risks and Methodology - VPEC

Valuation: We adopt a discounted P/E method and apply a 22.2x target P/E on VPEC’s
2029E EPS, discounting back to 2027E at a COE of 10.5% (beta 1.7x, risk free rate 1.6%
and market risk premium at 5.1%), to derive our 12m TP of NT$793. Our target P/E is
based on a 0.4x PEG&M ratio derived from the average level of peers in the photonics
supply chain.
Key risk: Slower-than-expected SiPh business ramp up; weaker-than-expected
microelectronics demand from smartphones clients; IDM offers EPI wafer in-house.

Price Target Risks and Methodology - RoboTechnik
Valuation: Our 12-month target price of Rmb796 is based on a 20.7x 2031E P/E,
discounted back to 2027E at a COE of 11.3%. Our target multiple is derived from the
correlation between consumer electronic peers’ PEG&M (2026E P/E to 2027E NI YoY and
OPM), with RoboTechnik’s 2032E net income at 2% YoY and 2032E OPM at 32%.
Key downside risks: Fiercer-than-expected market competition; lower-than-expected

optical connection end demand; worse-than-expected photovoltaic market downcycle.

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions
Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not Covered), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.
Coverage Universe: A list of all stocks in each coverage universe is available by primary analyst, stock and coverage universe at

https://www.gs.com/research/hedge.html.

In this report, we focus on Chenbro's monthly sales, which we
believe could be a catalyst for its share price.
Details:
• August  sales were NT$2,457mn (flat MoM/+41% YoY).
• QTD sales reached 59% of our 3Q26 estimate at NT$8,368mn (+7% QoQ/
+48% YoY) and 56% of consensus at NT$8,763mn (+12% QoQ/+55% YoY).
Our view:
• We see its 3Q26 revenue tracking slightly behind our estimate.
• We think near-term sentiment should remain lackluster amid the temporary

hiccup resulting from supply chain management.
• Management remains optimistic on the 2H26 outlook. The demand from its
GPU/ASIC server clusters and general servers is strong, driving  orders for its
chassis and rack offerings.

Valuation Methodology and Risks
Chenbro (8210.TW)
Base case, derived from 27x 2027e P/E. We view our target multiple as justified by the 33%
earnings CAGR we see for 2025-28, which implies a PEG of 0.8. This is in line the average of
0.8 over 2014-24, when the stock was trading at an average P/E of 12x for a 15% earnings
CAGR.
Risks to Upside
n Stronger AI capex outlook
n Larger market share gains within existing customers

n Faster penetration into new customers
n Higher entry barrier among peers that leads to higher ASP/profitability
n Better macro outlook
Risks to Downside
n Softer AI capex
n Less share gains within existing customers
n Slower penetration into new customers
n Intensifying competition leading to lower ASP/profitability
n Continuous macro uncertainties

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk -adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk -adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk -adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk -adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below .
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below .

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below .
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

MPI Corporation  | Asia Pacific
3Q Revenue Looks Better than
Feared; OW
MPI reported August sales of NT$2,048mn, +13% M/M and +70% Y/Y, above our
NT$1,876mn estimate –  mainly supported by price hikes, TPU, and other AI ASICs.
Assuming flat September revenue M/M, 3Q revenue would rise 13% Q/Q, above our
+7% estimate and the Street at +10% Q/Q.
Stay OW: As previously indicated, we expect 3Q revenue to exceed guidance given
CPC/VPC price hikes. With very strong micro bump demand and more migration to

2nm, we believe MPI needs to expand MEMS probe card capacity to at least 8–10mn
pins per month in 2027, positioning it to meet strong AI ASIC and GPU demand.
PCB material shortages remain a risk; however, MPI is diversifying suppliers and
seeking substitutes.
For CPO,  Insertion 2 qualification continues, with insertion 3 shipments on track to
start in 4Q26.
Investors were slightly frustrated by the conservative guidance, but still view MPI as
best positioned to benefit from increased chip probing demand over the longer term

Key Takeaways
The stock could come under pressure following management's conservative
guidance. However, we expect more updates to follow in coming quarters.
Strong micro bump demand and greater migration to 2nm lead the team to argue
that MEMS probe card capacity should expand to 8–10mn pins per month in
2027.
PCB material shortages remain an explicit risk. Mitigation centers on diversifying

suppliers/seeking substitutes to reduce reliance on constrained inputs.

Valuation Methodology and Risks
                        MPI Corporation (6223.TWO)                    
We derive our price target from a residual income model, as we believe this can best capture 
the long-term value. We assume a 9.78% cost of equity (risk-free rate 2.0% and risk premium 
6.0%), a payout ratio of 67%, a medium-term growth rate of 16.0%, and a terminal growth rate 
of 4.0%. Our price target implies 50x/25x 2027e/2028e P/E. We believe its valuation will con-
tinue to climb along with growing earnings. 
                        Risks to Upside                    
n Stronger-than-expected AI demand
n Stronger-than-expected market share gain from peers
n Stronger-than-expected CPO development

Risks to Downside                    
n Slower-than-expected AI demand
n Slower-than-expected market share gain from peers
n Slower-than-expected CPO development

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

季獲利走勢與年成長率

研究員/陳晏平 TINA.YP.CHEN@uni-psg.com

1H26 稅後淨利為 438 億元，成長 328%YOY，EPS 為 1.69 元。獲利
成長主要因:(1)台新銀核心收益皆逾兩成的成長 ， 該子公司獲利成
長 26%YOY 至 124 億元；(2)2025/7/24 完成合併新光金，新光銀、
元富證 1H26 合計額外貢獻 67 億元獲利，加上 2026 年接軌 IFRS 
17，壽險開始有穩定利差益，1H26 合併後壽險子公司的獲利為 225
億元。展望 2026~2027 年，由於:(1) 台新銀維持放款將高個位數
成長，並上修淨利差擴大幅度，上修 2026 年淨利息收益至成長
19%YOY，並預期 2027 年將成長 5%YOY；(2) 台新銀財管商品銷售
動能強勁，信用卡業務持續成長，預期 2026 年整體淨手續費收益
將雙位數成長 ，2027 年個位數增加 ；(3) 台新銀資產品質穩定 ， 但
為新增放款 1%/1.5%法定提存，預期2026~2027 年淨提存將增加六
成至 30 億元 ；(4) 新壽接軌 IFRS 17 後擁穩定利差益 ， 另有80~100
億元的收購價格分攤數額外挹注，推估 2026 年將貢獻獲利逾 400
億元 ，2027 年亦將持續穩增；(5) 台股價量齊揚 ， 帶動證券子公司
獲利明顯增長，但同時也墊高基期。因此，在全年的合併效果下，
預估台新新光金 2026~2027 年稅後淨利為 805 億元、798 億元，成
長 116%、 衰退1%YOY， 扣除特別股股利25 億元後 ，EPS 為 3.11 元、
3.08 元 。 投資建議方面 ， 雖2026 年獲利明顯跳增 ， 且新壽1H26 已
累積 512 億元 OCI 資本利得亦可為配息來源，預期 2027 年現金股
利將優於 2026 年的 1 元水準 ， 不過2026 年獲利提升有部份是來自
無實際現金流入的 PPA，加上人壽能否上繳股利至金控尚不明確，
因此，在銀行、證券子公司較能上繳股利情況下，現金股利提升幅
度恐將受限。另以2027 年每股淨值 30 元、ROE 10~11%來看，目前
股價已逾 1.3xPBR，評價相對合理，故對台新新光金持「中立」投
資建議。

重點摘要： 
1. 台新銀放款將高個位數成 長，並上修淨利差擴大幅度，上修
2026年淨利息收益至成長19%YOY， 並預期2027年將成長5%YOY 
1H26底台新銀放款餘額為2.027兆元，成長11%YTD、12%YOY，
主要成長動能來自法人金融 、 加值型房屋貸款 、 個人無擔信用
貸款，分別增加15.5%、10%、15%YOY，一般房屋貸款則 小增
2%YOY。利差方面，雖高利差外幣放款成長動能強勁，1H26外
幣放款成長33%YOY，有提高資金運用效率 ，但受到台灣市場資
金緊俏影響，導致資金成本上揚，因此，2Q26存放利差為

### Page 2

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  2

1.53%，縮小6bpsQOQ、擴大14bpsYOY，淨利差亦同步 縮小
6bpsQOQ、擴大13bpsYOY至1.42%。不過因放款YOY持續成長、
淨利差YOY亦呈現擴大趨勢，1H26台新銀淨利息收益為200億
元，成長27%YOY。

圖一、台新銀利差趨勢

資料來源： 公司、統一投顧彙整

展望2026~2027年，由於出口動能維持強勁，景氣持續成長，
台新銀維持原預期—法金放款雙位數增長 、個金放款穩健高
個位數成長 ， 預期台新銀2026年整體放款將高個位數成長 。但
因2027年即將與新光銀合併 ， 預期將有部分法金客戶重疊 ， 須
重新評估放款額度 ， 預期2027年放款成長性將會不如2026年。
利差方面 ， 雖近期台灣資金緊俏影響資金成本 ， 但因先前美國
降息刺激高利差外幣放款需求，在 1H26存放款結構調整優於
預期下，公司上修2026年淨利差擴大幅度至5~8bpsYOY(原預
期將擴大4~5bpsYOY)。 因此 ， 研究部上修台新銀2026年淨利息
收益2%至404億元，成長19%YOY，並預期2027年將續增5%YOY。

2. 台新銀財管商品銷售動能強勁，信用卡業務持續成長，預期
2026年整體淨手續費收益將雙位數成長，2027年個位數增加 
1H26台新銀整體淨手續費收益為110億元，成長32%YOY，主要
成長動能來自:(1)投資市場火熱刺激財富管理商品熱銷，整
體財富管理業務手續費收益成長32%YOY；(2)透過簽帳金額結
構改變(減少低/零手收)、控制權益支出，信用卡業務手續費
收入增加28%YOY；(3)隨企金放款增長，企金相關手續費增加
56%YOY。

### Page 3

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  3

展望2026~2027年，因投資市場持續位在高檔，仍有利財富管
理商品銷售 ， 因此 ， 公司維持2026年台新銀整體淨手續費收益
將持續雙位數成長的預期，研究部推估將落在210~215億元水
準 ， 成長逾兩成 。 而在基期墊高下 ， 研究部預期2027年整體淨
手續費收益將個位數穩增。

3. 台新銀資產品質穩定，但為新增放款1%/1.5%法定提存，預期
2026~2027年淨提存將增加六成至30億元 
2026年以來無大額不良壞帳產生 ， 台新銀1H26底逾放比 、 呆帳
覆蓋率分別為0.13%、923%，資產品質不錯，些微優於產業平
均逾放比0.14%、呆帳覆蓋率940%。提存方面，雖針對新增逾
放的額外提存不多 ， 但因放款成長動能不弱 ， 而有新增放款的
1%或1.5%正常提存需求 ， 台新銀1H26淨呆帳提存為25億元 ，增
加112%YOY。

圖二、台新銀資產品質趨勢與同業平均水準比較

資料來源： 公司、統一投顧彙整

展望2026~2027年，目前資產品質穩定，暫無潛在大型不良放
款產生 ， 但因仍持續有新增放款的1%或1.5%正常提存， 公司預
期台新銀2026年信用成本將在10~15bps區間(維持不變)，較
2025年的10bps略增。研究部按公司預期的區間中上緣進行推
估，估計台新銀2026~2027年淨呆帳提存為30億元，較2025年
增加近六成。

### Page 4

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  4

4. 新壽接軌IFRS 17後擁穩定利差益，另有80~100億元的收購價
格分攤數額外挹注，推估2026年將貢獻獲利逾400億元，2027
年持續穩增 
台新金與新光金合併案已在2025/7/24完成，台新金為存續公
司 ， 並更名為台新新光金 。 而旗下兩家投信子公司 、 兩家壽險
子公司、兩家證券子公司已分別在 2025/10/24、2026/1/1、
2026/4/6完成整併，並計畫2027/1/1完成兩家銀行子公司間
的整併。

其中 ， 兩家壽險子公司合併後 ， 台新人壽為存續公司 ， 但更名
為新光人壽。因2026年接軌IFRS 17，現階段壽險公司主要獲
利來源包括：保險服務結果、財務結果、其他營業結果。在保
險服務結果方面，1H26共釋出95億元CSM，加計RA釋出、實際
理賠及費用與預期的差異 ， 保險服務結果共140.5億元 。 此外 ，
美債殖利率仍位在高檔，新錢投資報酬率不差，且在
2025/7/24合併當下有針對投資資產進行重新評價，投資資產
規模因而明顯下降， 1H26避險前經常性收益率為 4.19%，較
2025年提升85bpsYOY。(若以同樣投資資產規模基礎，還原後
為3.43%，仍為提高趨勢) 另，受惠避險工具費用減降，避險
成本下滑至1.22%， 以及投資市場上漲 ， 多少帶動TPL投資部位
評價利益、處分利益增加，1H26避險後投資報酬率為3.73%。
(若加計OCI資本利得則為5.85%)負債成本則因接軌IFRS 17，
保單利息成本如預期降至2.3%，換算1H26利差益為1.43%，財
務結果貢獻179億元。再扣除其他營業結果、稅負等，1H26新
光人壽獲利為225億元。

展望2026~2027年，由於1H26新增351億元CSM(2026年目標為
700億元)，1H26底帳上CSM餘額仍有2936億元，若以1H26攤銷
率推估 ， 預期2026年將可釋出約200億元的CSM至損益表上。加
上公司預期經常性收益率可提升至 3.5%以上、保單成本亦有
望明顯降低 ， 往後可享有較穩定的利差益 ， 且持續有收購價格
分攤數(PPA)額外挹注(原先新壽AC項下的債券部位具有較大
額未實現損失 ， 在合併當下是採公允價值評價 ， 隨此些債券陸
續到期 ， 原本帳上未實現損失可望逐步攤銷出來 ， 並挹注至損
益表上，亦即為收購價格分攤數)，此次法說會公司揭露2026
年PPA將達80~100億元 ， 推估新壽2026年獲利將可逾400億元 。
而因新壽TPL部位佔比不高，且隨CSM穩定釋出，並持續有PPA
額外貢獻，預期2027年獲利將穩定成長。

### Page 5

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  5

至於新光銀方面，1H26在淨利息收益穩增3%YOY、淨手續費收
益大增25%YOY、合併相關費用明顯減少、淨提存略有增加下，
獲利顯著大增168%YOY至48億元 。展望2026~2027年 ， 公司預期
2026年放款將個位數成長 、 淨利差維持穩定 ， 信用成本亦維持
原先預期的15~18bps，但淨手續費收益將雙位數成長(原預期
為中個位數增長)，加上營業費用正常化，因此，研究部上修
新光銀2026年獲利貢獻達96億元水準。

5. 台新新光金2027年現金股利將有望優於2026年，但人壽能否
上繳獲利至金控仍未可知，現金股利提升幅度恐不如預期 
資本適足率方面 ，旗下的台新銀、 新光銀1H26底整體資本適足
率、第一類資本適足率各為 13.7%、12.0%，以及14.24%、
12.41%，資本充足無虞。而合併後的新光人壽在 2026年接軌
TIS 時，已向主管機關申請在 125% ，而 1H26 底 約 莫 落 在
125~130%區間水準，在法定下限—100%之上。

圖三、台新金、台新銀資本適足率情形

*金控整體資本適足率僅揭露至1Q26底 
資料來源： 公司、統一投顧彙整

股利政策方面 ，過去為保留資本因應業務成長 ，原台新金現金
股利配發率普遍落在35~50%區間水準 ， 另搭配些許股票股利 ，
整體股利配發率約莫在65~90%區間水準。但 因台新金為合併
新光金，台新新光金發行高達118.9億股的普通股來因應此次
合併案，使得台新新光金普通股股本較原先大幅膨脹九成 ， 因
此，為控制股本 、 提升股利競爭力 ，公司傾向往後股利政策將
以現金股利為主 ， 並會隨著獲利慢慢增加 ， 不會波動過大。雖：
(1)2026年在接軌IFRS 17後，新壽獲利已相對穩定；(2)前述
的收購價格分攤數(PPA)雖無實際現金流入，但依然可進行分
配；(3)2026年以來股市顯著上漲，1H26新壽已累積512億元

OCI股票資本利得，亦可成為分配來源，預期2027年現金股利

附錄 一 ： 評 等 之 標 準 
評等  定義  
強力買進     預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬超過 25%以上  
買進  預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬 10% 以上  
中立  預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬介於 10～-10%  
降低持股  預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬介於 (-10%)以下  
未評等  沒有足夠的基本資料判斷該公司評等

司 認 可 之 資 料來 源 ， 並 基 於 特 定 日 期 所 做 之 判 斷 ， 但 不 保 證 其 完 整 性 或 正 確 性 ， 報
告 中 所 有 的 意 見 及 預 估 ， 如 有 變 更 恕 不 另 行 通 知 。

本 研 究 報 告 所 載 之 投 資 資 訊 ， 僅 提 供 客 戶 做 為 一 般 投 資 參 考 ， 並 非 針 對 特 定 對 象 提
供 專 屬 之 投 資 建 議 。 文 中 所 載 資 訊 或 任 何 意 見 ， 不 構 成 任 何 買 賣 有 價 證 券 或 其 他 投
資 標 的 之 要 約 、 宣 傳 或 引 誘 等 事 項 。 對 於 本 投 資 報 告 所 討 論 或 建 議 之 任 何 證 券 、 投
資 標 的 ， 或 文 中 所 討 論 或 建 議 之 投 資 策 略 ， 投 資 人 應 就 其 是 否 適 合 本 身 財 務 狀 況 與
投 資 條 件 ， 進 一 步 諮 詢 財 務 顧 問 的 意 見 。 本 投 資 報 告 之 內 容 取 材 自 據 信 為 可 靠 之 資
料 來 源 ， 但 概 不 以 明 示 或 默 示 的 方 式 ， 對 資 料 之 準 確 性 、 完 整 性 或 正 確 性 作 出 任 何
陳 述 或 保 證 。 本 投 資 報 告 載 述 意 見 進 行 更 改 與 撤 回 並 不 另 行 通 知 。 本 投 資 報 告 並 非
（且不應 解 釋 為 ）在 任 何 司 法 管 轄 區 內 ， 任 何 非 依 法 從 事 證 券 經 紀 或 交 易 之 人 士 或
公 司 ， 為 於 該 管 轄 區 內 從 事 證 券 經 紀 或 交 易 之 遊 說 。 本 投 資 報 告 內 容 屬 統 一 投 顧 之
著 作 權 ， 嚴 禁 抄 襲 與 仿 造 。

順利 2027 年第 2 季可望開始出貨。

正達 產品為 玻 璃 的 精 密 加
工，主 要 業 務 為 光電 、車 用 、
建築 等應用  
 正達(3149) 成立於 1996 年 6 月，2011 年 11 月掛牌上市 ，為台灣最大的光電玻
璃加工服務供應商，擁有玻璃切割、強化、薄化、鍍膜等完整加工服務。總部位
於台灣苗栗，生產基地位於台灣苗栗、中國觀瀾及成都、越南。主要產品包括：
保護玻璃、光學鍍膜玻璃、ITO 鍍膜玻璃，產品應用於蘋果電腦及平板、汽車、
建築。主要客戶為蘋果、藍思、群創、GIS-KY。正達與美商康寧簽署車載玻璃
戰略合作備忘錄，在汽車座艙與顯示應用上，結合上游玻璃材料與下游加工能
力，有利守住車用高階市場，並同步受惠國際品牌導入新設計 。

預期 3Q26 毛利率優於前期，加
以認列廠房處分利益，EPS 2.07
元 
 正達 2Q26 營收 5.54 億元，-6.1%QoQ，-2.1%YoY，營收下滑主要是因蘋果議
價導致，毛利率從前其-4.1%升至-3.2%，營業費用為 0.89 億元，營業損失約為
1.07 億元，稅後淨損 1.08 億元，EPS -0.48 元。展望 26H2，正達表示營運高
峰有望在 3Q26，且閒置的銅鑼三廠出售預期在 3Q26 完成交割。整體而言，投
顧估計正達 3Q26 營收 6.28 億元，+13.2%QoQ，+6.1%YoY，毛利率回升，加
以認列銅鑼三廠處分利益，EPS 2.07 元。

正達表示 2026 年成長動能主要來自智能汽車 產品，智能建築及智能光電將下
滑；預期今年底智能汽車營收占比將從目前的 14%升至 20%。整體而言，正達
預期 2026 年營收可維持小幅成長局面，且毛利率受到產品組合優化，預計將持
續上升，投顧預期正達 2026 年營收 23.83 億元，年增 4.4%。

HDD 需 求 夯，正 達 積 極 拚 擴
產  
 正達近期積極切入 AI、無人機、半導體等高毛利率領域。隨 AI 伺服器帶動 3.5 
吋 HDD 需求，HAMR 對高溫玻璃基板的需求，未來大容量 HDD 以玻璃碟盤
為主。正達以超低平坦 度拋光、奈米級剪薄、 3D 曲面鍍膜等技術切入， 目前
已建置月產能 10 萬片，預計 2026 年底擴至月產能 100 萬片，2027 年第四季
達月產能 500 萬片，2028 年過後若滿載年產值約達 60 億元，以淨利率 15%為
假設，可貢獻 EPS 3~4 元。

至於軍工與無人機領域 與美國國防系統整合商、北約系統商簽訂多項框架協
議，合作航太軍工級玻璃適形天線與軍用無人機，產品已在無人機、無人船實

2026.09.09 
徐文華 C00215@ibfs.com.tw 
3324.TT 雙鴻 
 水冷板重回 NVIDIA Vera Rubin 供應鏈

[財務資訊更新]

公司簡介 
雙鴻成立於 1998 年 8 月，主要生產散熱器、散熱片、散熱模組、散熱導管、風扇等，
應用於筆記型電腦 、 桌上型電腦 、 伺服器 、 投影機等領域 ， 主要終端客戶為Apple(AAPL 
US)、Dell(DELL US)、Samsung(005930 KS)、HP(HPQ US)、 宏碁(2353)、 華碩(2357)
等。 
投資評等/目標價 重點摘要 
買進 1,620 
【 前 次 投 資 建 議 】區 間 操 作，1,260 
【大盤指數/股價】47106 / 1340

➢ 2H26 新品逐步放量，營運動能增溫  
雙鴻 8 月營收 31.4 億元，MoM-16.6%，YoY+67.2%， 營收月減是受客
戶拉貨排程影響，以及 7 月基期較高所致。雙鴻 1H26 伺服器營收占比
已突破 70%(水冷營收占比已達 55%)，3Q26 為新舊產品交替期，整體
2H26 在 ASIC 與 GPU 伺服器的強勢帶動下，營運展望樂觀，公司維持
全年營收 YoY+70%的目標不變： 
1)隨著搭載新一代晶片的 AISC 伺服器問世，ASIC 散熱方案預期會在
4Q26~1Q27 明顯推升雙鴻業績 ， 且ASIC 客戶持續擴大水冷散熱導入 ，
此部分效益可挹注到 2027 年；2)雙鴻 3Q26 cold plate 出貨以 GB300
為大宗 ， 近期宣布其Vera Rubin cold plate， 已回到NVIDIA 推薦名單 ，
預期 4Q26 小量出貨；3)其他新品方面，DIMM cold plate 已於 2Q26
小量出貨。CPU 均熱片 2026 年營收占比為個位數，要到 2027~2028
年效益才會較明顯。 
➢ 2027 年泰國持續擴產 
雙鴻生產基地位於台灣、中國(廣州/昆山/重慶/合肥)、泰國，泰國廠為
近年擴產重點 ：1)泰國一期廠區已於 2025 年投產 ， 主要生產cold plate、
manifold、Rack CDU 等液冷散熱元件，目前產能為 cold plate 250k/
月、manifold 3k/ 月；2)泰國二期於 2026 年 9 月正式量產，主要生產
cold plate 、manifold，並預留電源水冷產能，若各產品線產能順利拉
升 ， 未來泰國各項產品產能可翻倍 ；3)泰國三期規劃在 4Q26 動工 、2H27
投產；4)泰國四期預計 2028 年動工。 
➢ 建議調升至買進，目標價 1,620 元  
雙鴻 2H26 營運展望正向，AI 伺服器的強勢增長，能彌補消費性電子市
況低迷帶來的不利影響。 3Q26 GB 300 散熱產品出貨持續增量，並且
cold plate 已重新回到 NVIDIA Vera Rubin 推薦名單，預期 4Q26 小量
出貨，而 ASIC 客戶之新品亦逐漸放量中，有利營運回溫。雙鴻歷史 PE
區間為 8X~36X， 考量新品可逐步帶動2H26~1H27 之業績 ， 且目前PE
評價偏低，建議逢低買進，目標價 1,620 元(2027F PE 20X)。
